As such, addressing this gap is critical to ensure that the cipher's modifications do not unintentionally reduce its security margin. Therefore, this paper leverages Knuth-Morris-Pratt (\texttt{KMP}) and Boyer-Moore (\texttt{BM}) algorithms to analyze \texttt{EChaCha20}, which is a variant of ChaCha20 that features an expanded 6$\times$6 state matrix and an enhanced \texttt{QR-F}. The author has developed and optimized adaptations of the \texttt{KMP} and \texttt{BM} algorithms for 32-bit word level pattern analysis and employed them to investigate $m$-bit pattern frequency distributions to assess the \texttt{EChaCha20}'s resistance of rotational-differential attacks. Our experimental results on large-scale one million keystream datasets have confirmed that \texttt{EChaCha20} is able to maintain strong pseudorandomness at 16-bit and 32-bit levels with minor irregularities observed in the 8-bit domain. In addition to these, the differential tests have indicated a rapid diffusion, exhibiting an avalanche effect after two \texttt{QR-F} rounds and no statistically significant rotational collisions were observed within the evaluated bounds, consistent with expected ARX diffusion behavior beyond 3 rounds.
  This work puts forward SBC as a complementary tool for ARX cipher evaluation and provide new thoughts on the security properties of \texttt{EChaCha20}.

</details>


### [112] [Conversations Risk Detection LLMs in Financial Agents via Multi-Stage Generative Rollout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09056)
*Xiaotong Jiang,Jun Wu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FinSec：面向金融智能体的四层安全检测框架，通过结构化、可解释的端到端风险识别，显著提升高风险对话检测的鲁棒性，在保持模型效用的同时实现领先的安全性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型在金融服务场景的快速应用，高监管风险下的对话安全检测面临重大挑战。现有方法主要依赖单维度语义判断或固定规则，难以处理多轮语义演化和复杂监管条款，且缺乏专门针对金融安全检测的模型。

Method: 提出FinSec四层安全检测框架：1)可疑行为模式分析，2)延迟风险和对抗推理，3)语义安全分析，4)基于风险的综合决策。该框架实现结构化、可解释的端到端金融风险识别。

Result: FinSec在整体检测能力上达到F1分数90.13%，比基线模型提升6-14个百分点；ASR降至9.09%，显著降低不安全输出概率；AUPRC提升至0.9189，比通用框架提高约9.7%。在效用与安全平衡方面，综合得分0.9098。

Conclusion: FinSec框架显著增强了高风险对话检测的鲁棒性，同时保持模型效用，为金融智能体对话提供了稳健高效的保护方案，在金融安全检测领域具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: With the rapid adoption of large language models (LLMs) in financial service scenarios, dialogue security detection under high regulatory risk presents significant challenges. Existing methods mainly rely on single-dimensional semantic judgments or fixed rules, making them inadequate for handling multi-turn semantic evolution and complex regulatory clauses; moreover, they lack models specifically designed for financial security detection. To address these issues, this paper proposes FinSec, a four-tier security detection framework for financial agent. FinSec enables structured, interpretable, and end-to-end identification of actual financial risks, incorporating suspicious behavior pattern analysis, delayed risk and adversarial inference, semantic security analysis, and integrated risk-based decision-making. Notably, FinSec significantly enhances the robustness of high-risk dialogue detection while maintaining model utility. Experimental results demonstrate FinSec's leading performance. In terms of overall detection capability, FinSec achieves an F1 score of 90.13%, improving upon baseline models by 6--14 percentage points; its ASR is reduced to 9.09%, markedly lowering the probability of unsafe outputs; and the AUPRC increases to 0.9189 -- an approximate 9.7% gain over general frameworks. Additionally, in balancing utility and safety, FinSec obtains a composite score of 0.9098, delivering robust and efficient protection for financial agent dialogues.

</details>


### [113] [CLIP-Inspector: Model-Level Backdoor Detection for Prompt-Tuned CLIP via OOD Trigger Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09101)
*Akshit Jindal,Saket Anand,Chetan Arora,Vikram Goyal*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CLIP-Inspector (CI) 是一种针对提示调优CLIP模型的后门检测方法，能够在仅使用1000张未标记OOD图像的情况下，通过重构潜在触发器来检测恶意提供商植入的后门，检测准确率达94%，并能通过微调修复模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着组织将模型训练外包给MLaaS提供商，恶意提供商可能在遵循提示调优协议的同时植入后门，使触发输入被分类到攻击者选择的类别。现有方法主要关注编码器损坏，无法检测这种仅通过提示调优植入的后门，数据级方法也无法回答"交付的模型是否被后门攻击"这一关键问题。

Method: CLIP-Inspector (CI) 假设对交付模型具有白盒访问权限，并拥有未标记的OOD图像池。该方法为每个类别重构可能的触发器，通过分析模型是否表现出后门行为来进行检测。具体通过单轮迭代使用1000张OOD图像来重构有效触发器。

Result: 在10个数据集和4种后门攻击的广泛实验中，CI仅使用1000张OOD图像就能在单个epoch内重构有效触发器，达到94%的检测准确率（50个模型中检测出47个）。与适应的触发器反转基线相比，CI的AUROC得分显著更高（0.973 vs 0.495/0.687）。

Conclusion: CLIP-Inspector能够有效检测提示调优CLIP模型中的后门，解决了现有方法无法检测这类后门的问题。此外，使用CI重构的触发器对正确标记的触发输入进行微调，可以重新对齐模型并降低后门有效性，从而实现对提示调优CLIP模型的审查和事后修复，确保安全部署。

Abstract: Organisations with limited data and computational resources increasingly outsource model training to Machine Learning as a Service (MLaaS) providers, who adapt vision-language models (VLMs) such as CLIP to downstream tasks via prompt tuning rather than training from scratch. This semi-honest setting creates a security risk where a malicious provider can follow the prompt-tuning protocol yet implant a backdoor, forcing triggered inputs to be classified into an attacker-chosen class, even for out-of-distribution (OOD) data. Such backdoors leave encoders untouched, making them undetectable to existing methods that focus on encoder corruption. Other data-level methods that sanitize data before training or during inference, also fail to answer the critical question, "Is the delivered model backdoored or not?" To address this model-level verification problem, we introduce CLIP-Inspector (CI), a backdoor detection method designed for prompt-tuned CLIP models. Assuming white-box access to the delivered model and a pool of unlabeled OOD images, CI reconstructs possible triggers for each class to determine if the model exhibits backdoor behaviour or not. Additionally, we demonstrate that using CI's reconstructed trigger for fine-tuning on correctly labeled triggered inputs enables us to re-align the model and reduce backdoor effectiveness. Through extensive experiments across ten datasets and four backdoor attacks, we demonstrate that CI can reconstruct effective triggers in a single epoch using only 1,000 OOD images, achieving a 94% detection accuracy (47/50 models). Compared to adapted trigger-inversion baselines, CI yields a markedly higher AUROC score (0.973 vs 0.495/0.687), thus enabling the vetting and post-hoc repair of prompt-tuned CLIP models to ensure safe deployment.

</details>


### [114] [Unreal Thinking: Chain-of-Thought Hijacking via Two-stage Backdoor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09235)
*Wenhan Chang,Tianqing Zhu,Ping Xiong,Faqian Guan,Wanlei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种针对大型语言模型思维链（CoT）的新型安全攻击方法——两阶段后门劫持（TSBH），通过逆向合成恶意思维链并注入后门，使模型在特定触发条件下输出恶意推理过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型语言模型在需要思维链解释的场景中部署，攻击者可能通过操纵模型的可观察思维链来实施恶意行为。在开源权重生态系统中，这种操纵可以通过轻量级适配器实现，易于分发和附加到基础模型上。然而，现有的持久性思维链劫持面临三个主要挑战：难以在连续的思维链输出序列中直接劫持思维链令牌同时保持稳定的下游输出、恶意思维链数据稀缺、以及朴素后门注入方法的不稳定性。

Method: 论文提出多重逆向树搜索（MRTS）来解决数据稀缺问题，这是一种逆向合成过程，从提示-输出对构建输出对齐的思维链，而无需直接从对齐模型中引出恶意思维链。基于MRTS，引入两阶段后门劫持（TSBH）：第一阶段诱导触发条件导致的中间思维链与恶意输出之间的不匹配；第二阶段在MRTS生成的思维链上微调模型，这些思维链与恶意输出具有较低的嵌入距离，从而确保更强的语义相似性。

Result: 在多个开源权重模型上的实验表明，该方法成功诱导了触发激活的思维链劫持，同时在评估框架下保持了劫持状态与基线状态之间的可量化区别。论文还探索了基于推理的缓解方法，并发布了安全推理数据集以支持未来关于安全感知和可靠推理的研究。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了大型语言模型思维链推理的新安全风险，并提出了一种有效的攻击方法。同时，通过提供安全推理数据集和探索缓解策略，为未来构建更安全、可靠的推理系统奠定了基础。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in settings where Chain-of-Thought (CoT) is interpreted by users. This creates a new safety risk: attackers may manipulate the model's observable CoT to make malicious behaviors. In open-weight ecosystems, such manipulation can be embedded in lightweight adapters that are easy to distribute and attach to base models. In practice, persistent CoT hijacking faces three main challenges: the difficulty of directly hijacking CoT tokens within one continuous long CoT-output sequence while maintaining stable downstream outputs, the scarcity of malicious CoT data, and the instability of naive backdoor injection methods. To address the data scarcity issue, we propose Multiple Reverse Tree Search (MRTS), a reverse synthesis procedure that constructs output-aligned CoTs from prompt-output pairs without directly eliciting malicious CoTs from aligned models. Building on MRTS, we introduce Two-stage Backdoor Hijacking (TSBH), which first induces a trigger-conditioned mismatch between intermediate CoT and malicious outputs, and then fine-tunes the model on MRTS-generated CoTs that have lower embedding distance to the malicious outputs, thereby ensuring stronger semantic similarity. Experiments across multiple open-weight models demonstrate that our method successfully induces trigger-activated CoT hijacking while maintaining a quantifiable distinction between hijacked and baseline states under our evaluation framework. We further explore a reasoning-based mitigation approach and release a safety-reasoning dataset to support future research on safety-aware and reliable reasoning. Our code is available at https://github.com/ChangWenhan/TSBH_official.

</details>


### [115] [Cross-Paradigm Models of Restricted Syndrome Decoding with Application to CROSS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09292)
*Étienne Burle,Aleksei Udovenko*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文将受限伴随式解码(ResSD)问题归约到基于码和格的问题，分析了CROSS签名方案的安全性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ResSD是CROSS后量子签名方案的基础安全假设，需要深入分析其安全性以评估该NIST候选方案

Method: 将ResSD问题归约到码基问题(正则伴随式解码)和格基问题(最近向量问题、短/近向量列表)，通过构造具有特定结构和小范数向量的新码

Result: 成功将ResSD归约到码基和格基问题，扩展了攻击面，在缩减参数上进行了理论和实验评估

Conclusion: ResSD的安全性与码基和格基问题相关，为CROSS签名方案的安全性提供了新的见解

Abstract: Restricted Syndrome Decoding (ResSD) is a variant of linear code decoding problem where each of the error's entries must belong to a fixed small set of values. This problem underlies the security of CROSS, a post-quantum signature scheme that is one of the Round 2 candidates of NIST's ongoing additional signatures call. We show that solutions to this problem can be deduced from vectors of a particular structure and a small norm in newly constructed codes, in both Hamming and Euclidean metrics. This allows us to reduce Restricted Syndrome Decoding to both code-based (Regular Syndrome Decoding) and lattice-based problems (Closest Vector Problem, List of Short/Close Vectors), increasing the attack surface and providing new insights into the security of ResSD. We evaluate our attacks on CROSS instances both theoretically and experimentally on reduced parameters.

</details>


### [116] [BadSkill: Backdoor Attacks on Agent Skills via Model-in-Skill Poisoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09378)
*Guiyao Tie,Jiawen Shi,Pan Zhou,Lichao Sun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: BadSkill攻击：针对AI智能体生态系统中第三方技能嵌入模型的供应链后门攻击，通过看似良性的技能隐藏恶意模型，在特定语义触发条件下激活隐藏载荷


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体生态系统依赖可安装技能扩展功能，一些技能将学习模型作为执行逻辑的一部分。这带来了供应链风险：第三方技能可能表面良性，但在其捆绑模型中隐藏恶意行为。现有研究未充分捕捉这种模型在技能中的威胁面。

Method: 提出BadSkill后门攻击框架：1）发布看似良性的技能，其嵌入模型经过后门微调；2）使用复合目标函数训练分类器，结合分类损失、基于边界的分离和毒化聚焦优化；3）在OpenClaw启发的仿真环境中评估，保留第三方技能安装和执行功能；4）基准测试涵盖13个技能（8个触发任务+5个非触发控制技能），共571个负类查询和396个触发对齐查询。

Result: 在5个模型家族的8种架构（494M-7.1B参数）上，BadSkill在8个触发技能上达到最高99.5%平均攻击成功率（ASR），同时在负类查询上保持强良性准确率。在标准测试分割的毒化率扫描中，3%毒化率即可获得91.7% ASR。攻击在评估的模型规模和5种文本扰动类型下保持有效。

Conclusion: 研究识别了智能体生态系统中模型承载技能作为独特的模型供应链风险，需要更强的来源验证和行为审查机制来应对第三方技能工件的安全威胁。

Abstract: Agent ecosystems increasingly rely on installable skills to extend functionality, and some skills bundle learned model artifacts as part of their execution logic. This creates a supply-chain risk that is not captured by prompt injection or ordinary plugin misuse: a third-party skill may appear benign while concealing malicious behavior inside its bundled model. We present BadSkill, a backdoor attack formulation that targets this model-in-skill threat surface. In BadSkill, an adversary publishes a seemingly benign skill whose embedded model is backdoor-fine-tuned to activate a hidden payload only when routine skill parameters satisfy attacker-chosen semantic trigger combinations. To realize this attack, we train the embedded classifier with a composite objective that combines classification loss, margin-based separation, and poison-focused optimization, and evaluate it in an OpenClaw-inspired simulation environment that preserves third-party skill installation and execution while enabling controlled multi-model study. Our benchmark spans 13 skills, including 8 triggered tasks and 5 non-trigger control skills, with a combined main evaluation set of 571 negative-class queries and 396 trigger-aligned queries. Across eight architectures (494M--7.1B parameters) from five model families, BadSkill achieves up to 99.5\% average attack success rate (ASR) across the eight triggered skills while maintaining strong benign-side accuracy on negative-class queries. In poison-rate sweeps on the standard test split, a 3\% poison rate already yields 91.7\% ASR. The attack remains effective across the evaluated model scales and under five text perturbation types. These findings identify model-bearing skills as a distinct model supply-chain risk in agent ecosystems and motivate stronger provenance verification and behavioral vetting for third-party skill artifacts.

</details>


### [117] [XFED: Non-Collusive Model Poisoning Attack Against Byzantine-Robust Federated Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09489)
*Israt Jahan Mouri,Muhammad Ridowan,Muhammad Abdullah Adnan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: XFED：首个无需攻击者间通信的非共谋模型投毒攻击，在联邦学习中仅凭共享对抗目标即可绕过现有防御


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习模型投毒攻击依赖攻击者间的共谋协调，这在现实部署中不切实际且易被检测。研究需要探索无需通信的非共谋攻击模型是否可行。

Method: 提出非共谋攻击模型，攻击者共享对抗目标但独立操作。设计XFED攻击方法，无需攻击者间通信、不访问其他客户端更新、不依赖服务器防御知识，具有聚合不可知性。

Result: 在六个基准数据集上的实验表明，XFED能绕过八种最先进的防御方法，性能优于六种现有模型投毒攻击，证明非共谋攻击的可行性。

Conclusion: 联邦学习系统安全性远低于预期，非共谋模型投毒攻击构成严重威胁，迫切需要更鲁棒实用的防御机制。

Abstract: Model poisoning attacks pose a significant security threat to Federated Learning (FL). Most existing model poisoning attacks rely on collusion, requiring adversarial clients to coordinate by exchanging local benign models and synchronizing the generation of their poisoned updates. However, sustaining such coordination is increasingly impractical in real-world FL deployments, as it effectively requires botnet-like control over many devices. This approach is costly to maintain and highly vulnerable to detection. This context raises a fundamental question: Can model poisoning attacks remain effective without any communication between attackers? To address this challenge, we introduce and formalize the \textbf{non-collusive attack model}, in which all compromised clients share a common adversarial objective but operate independently. Under this model, each attacker generates its malicious update without communicating with other adversaries, accessing other clients' updates, or relying on any knowledge of server-side defenses. To demonstrate the feasibility of this threat model, we propose \textbf{XFED}, the first aggregation-agnostic, non-collusive model poisoning attack. Our empirical evaluation across six benchmark datasets shows that XFED bypasses eight state-of-the-art defenses and outperforms six existing model poisoning attacks. These findings indicate that FL systems are substantially less secure than previously believed and underscore the urgent need for more robust and practical defense mechanisms.

</details>


### [118] [Trans-RAG: Query-Centric Vector Transformation for Secure Cross-Organizational Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09541)
*Yu Liu,Kun Peng,Wenxiao Zhang,Fangfang Yuan,Cong Cao,Wenxuan Lu,Yanbing Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Trans-RAG提出了一种新的向量空间语言范式，通过向量空间隔离和查询转换实现跨组织RAG系统的安全、准确和高效检索，无需解密开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨组织部署的RAG系统面临安全、准确性和效率之间的基本矛盾。现有加密方法在解密时暴露明文，而联邦架构无法整合资源且开销巨大。

Method: 提出Trans-RAG系统，采用向量空间语言范式，每个组织的知识存在于数学隔离的语义空间。核心是vector2Trans多阶段转换技术，使查询能够通过查询中心转换动态"说"每个组织的向量空间"语言"，消除解密开销同时保持原生检索效率。

Result: 安全评估显示接近正交的向量空间（89.90°角度分离）和99.81%的隔离率。在8个检索器、3个数据集和3个LLM上的实验显示准确性下降最小（nDCG@10下降3.5%），相比同态加密有显著效率提升。

Conclusion: Trans-RAG通过创新的向量空间隔离和查询转换技术，有效解决了跨组织RAG系统的安全、准确性和效率矛盾，为安全的多组织知识检索提供了新范式。

Abstract: Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG) systems deployed across organizational boundaries face fundamental tensions between security, accuracy, and efficiency. Current encryption methods expose plaintext during decryption, while federated architectures prevent resource integration and incur substantial overhead. We introduce Trans-RAG, implementing a novel vector space language paradigm where each organization's knowledge exists in a mathematically isolated semantic space. At the core lies vector2Trans, a multi-stage transformation technique that enables queries to dynamically "speak" each organization's vector space "language" through query-centric transformations, eliminating decryption overhead while maintaining native retrieval efficiency. Security evaluations demonstrate near-orthogonal vector spaces with 89.90° angular separation and 99.81% isolation rates. Experiments across 8 retrievers, 3 datasets, and 3 LLMs show minimal accuracy degradation (3.5% decrease in nDCG@10) and significant efficiency improvements over homomorphic encryption.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [Spectral-Transport Stability and Benign Overfitting in Interpolating Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08625)
*Gustav Olaf Yunus Laitinen-Lundström Fredriksson-Imanov*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一个谱-输运稳定性理论框架，用于分析插值学习器的泛化性能，解释过参数化模型如何实现零经验风险同时保持预测准确性，并刻画良性过拟合与破坏性过拟合的边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究统计学习的插值区域中泛化性能的理论基础，解释为什么高度过参数化的估计器能够达到零经验风险的同时仍保持非平凡的预测准确性，以及如何刻画良性过拟合与破坏性过拟合的边界。

Method: 引入谱-输运稳定性框架，其中超额风险由数据分布的谱几何、学习规则在单样本替换下的敏感性（输运稳定性）以及标签噪声的对齐结构共同控制。提出了尺度依赖的Fredriksson指数，将有效维度、输运稳定性和噪声对齐结合为插值估计器的单一复杂度参数。

Result: 证明了有限样本风险界，通过Fredriksson指数在容许谱尺度上的消失建立了尖锐的良性过拟合准则，并在多项式谱衰减下推导了显式的相变速率。针对多项式谱线性插值的特定模型，获得了显式定理和相应的速率证明。

Conclusion: 该框架将算法稳定性、双重下降、良性过拟合、算子理论学习理论和隐式偏差统一到一个现代插值的结构化解释中，并通过展示优化动力学如何选择最小谱-输运能量的插值解来阐明隐式正则化机制。

Abstract: We develop a theoretical framework for generalization in the interpolating regime of statistical learning. The central question is why highly overparameterized estimators can attain zero empirical risk while still achieving nontrivial predictive accuracy, and how to characterize the boundary between benign and destructive overfitting. We introduce a spectral-transport stability framework in which excess risk is controlled jointly by the spectral geometry of the data distribution, the sensitivity of the learning rule under single-sample replacement, and the alignment structure of label noise. This leads to a scale-dependent Fredriksson index that combines effective dimension, transport stability, and noise alignment into a single complexity parameter for interpolating estimators. We prove finite-sample risk bounds, establish a sharp benign-overfitting criterion through the vanishing of the index along admissible spectral scales, and derive explicit phase-transition rates under polynomial spectral decay. For a model-specific specialization, we obtain an explicit theorem for polynomial-spectrum linear interpolation, together with a proof of the resulting rate. The framework also clarifies implicit regularization by showing how optimization dynamics can select interpolating solutions of minimal spectral-transport energy. These results connect algorithmic stability, double descent, benign overfitting, operator-theoretic learning theory, and implicit bias within a unified structural account of modern interpolation.

</details>


### [120] [Online Quantile Regression for Nonparametric Additive Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08969)
*Haoran Zhan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出投影函数梯度下降算法(P-FGD)用于在线非参数加性分位数回归训练，扩展了函数随机梯度下降框架到pinball损失，具有无需存储历史数据、计算复杂度低、预测速度快等优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线学习环境中需要高效训练非参数加性分位数回归模型，现有方法如RKHS在计算复杂度和存储需求方面存在不足，需要开发更高效的在线算法。

Method: 提出投影函数梯度下降算法(P-FGD)，将函数随机梯度下降框架扩展到pinball损失，利用希尔伯特空间投影恒等式，支持在线学习和小批量学习。

Result: P-FGD每步计算复杂度为O(J_t ln J_t)，预测时间为O(J_t)，无需存储历史数据，分位数函数估计器达到极小极大最优一致性速率O(t^{-2s/(2s+1)})。

Conclusion: P-FGD算法在在线非参数加性分位数回归中优于传统RKHS方法，具有计算高效、存储需求低、理论保证强的优势，适用于在线学习场景。

Abstract: This paper introduces a projected functional gradient descent algorithm (P-FGD) for training nonparametric additive quantile regression models in online settings. This algorithm extends the functional stochastic gradient descent framework to the pinball loss. An advantage of P-FGD is that it does not need to store historical data while maintaining $O(J_t\ln J_t)$ computational complexity per step where $J_t$ denotes the number of basis functions. Besides, we only need $O(J_t)$ computational time for quantile function prediction at time $t$. These properties show that P-FGD is much better than the commonly used RKHS in online learning. By leveraging a novel Hilbert space projection identity, we also prove that the proposed online quantile function estimator (P-FGD) achieves the minimax optimal consistency rate $O(t^{-\frac{2s}{2s+1}})$ where $t$ is the current time and $s$ denotes the smoothness degree of the quantile function. Extensions to mini-batch learning are also established.

</details>


### [121] [A Predictive View on Streaming Hidden Markov Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09208)
*Gerardo Duran-Martin*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向预测的流式隐马尔可夫模型优化框架，通过预算约束下的预测分布空间投影实现高效在线推理


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法在完全生成模型下追求完整后验恢复，但流式场景中精确滤波因状态路径指数增长而不可行，需要更高效的在线推理方法

Method: 将流式推理建模为预测分布空间中的约束投影问题：在固定假设预算下，通过前向KL最优的S路径混合来近似完整后验预测分布，得到重归一化的top-S后验加权混合解

Result: 算法完全递归且确定性，无需EM或采样，在匹配计算预算下与Online EM和Sequential Monte Carlo相比展现出竞争力的预测性能

Conclusion: 为HMM提供了一种基于预测优先原则的束搜索理论推导，实现了高效在线推理，平衡了计算复杂度和预测准确性

Abstract: We develop a predictive-first optimisation framework for streaming hidden Markov models. Unlike classical approaches that prioritise full posterior recovery under a fully specified generative model, we assume access to regime-specific predictive models whose parameters are learned online while maintaining a fixed transition prior over regimes. Our objective is to sequentially identify latent regimes while maintaining accurate step-ahead predictive distributions. Because the number of possible regime paths grows exponentially, exact filtering is infeasible. We therefore formulate streaming inference as a constrained projection problem in predictive-distribution space: under a fixed hypothesis budget, we approximate the full posterior predictive by the forward-KL optimal mixture supported on $S$ paths. The solution is the renormalised top-$S$ posterior-weighted mixture, providing a principled derivation of beam search for HMMs. The resulting algorithm is fully recursive and deterministic, performing beam-style truncation with closed-form predictive updates and requiring neither EM nor sampling. Empirical comparisons against Online EM and Sequential Monte Carlo under matched computational budgets demonstrate competitive prequential performance.

</details>


### [122] [Iterative Identification Closure: Amplifying Causal Identifiability in Linear SEMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09309)
*Ziyi Ding,Xiao-Ping Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出迭代识别闭合(IIC)框架，通过种子函数和简化HTC传播，将因果识别分解为两阶段，利用迭代反馈机制显著提升线性结构方程模型中因果效应的可识别性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统半路径准则(HTC)存在节点级局限性，导致15-23%的因果效应无法确定。现有图形准则孤立处理每条边或节点，缺乏利用已识别信息进一步解锁新识别的迭代反馈机制。

Method: IIC框架包含：(1)种子函数S_0从任意外部信息源识别初始边集；(2)简化HTC传播迭代替代已知系数降低系统维度。核心创新是迭代识别传播机制，新识别边反馈解锁进一步识别，需证明简化雅可比矩阵保持通用满秩（简化HTC定理）。

Result: IIC被证明是可靠的、单调的，在O(|E|)次迭代内收敛（经验上≤2次）。严格包含HTC和祖先分解，在n≤5的所有图上验证100%精确度（零假阳性）。结合种子时，IIC将HTC间隙减少超过80%，传播增益γ~4倍（2个种子识别~3%边到97.5%总识别）。

Conclusion: IIC通过迭代反馈机制显著提升因果效应识别能力，填补了HTC的识别间隙，为线性结构方程模型中的因果识别提供了更强大的通用框架。

Abstract: The Half-Trek Criterion (HTC) is the primary graphical tool for determining generic identifiability of causal effect coefficients in linear structural equation models (SEMs) with latent confounders. However, HTC is inherently node-wise: it simultaneously resolves all incoming edges of a node, leaving a gap of "inconclusive" causal effects (15-23% in moderate graphs). We introduce Iterative Identification Closure (IIC), a general framework that decouples causal identification into two phases: (1) a seed function S_0 that identifies an initial set of edges from any external source of information (instrumental variables, interventions, non-Gaussianity, prior knowledge, etc.); and (2) Reduced HTC propagation that iteratively substitutes known coefficients to reduce system dimension, enabling identification of edges that standard HTC cannot resolve. The core novelty is iterative identification propagation: newly identified edges feed back to unlock further identification -- a mechanism absent from all existing graphical criteria, which treat each edge (or node) in isolation. This propagation is non-trivial: coefficient substitution alters the covariance structure, and soundness requires proving that the modified Jacobian retains generic full rank -- a new theoretical result (Reduced HTC Theorem). We prove that IIC is sound, monotone, converges in O(|E|) iterations (empirically <=2), and strictly subsumes both HTC and ancestor decomposition. Exhaustive verification on all graphs with n<=5 (134,144 edges) confirms 100% precision (zero false positives); with combined seeds, IIC reduces the HTC gap by over 80%. The propagation gain is gamma~4x (2 seeds identifying ~3% of edges to 97.5% total identification), far exceeding gamma<=1.2x of prior methods that incorporate side information without iterative feedback.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [123] [Planted clique detection and recovery from the hypergraph adjacency matrix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08691)
*Kalle Alaluusua,B. R. Vinay Kumar*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 该论文研究了超图数据投影到加权图后的团检测与恢复问题，证明了在仅观测邻接矩阵的情况下，谱范数检验和谱方法在√n尺度下具有渐近最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超图数据通常被投影到加权图上，通过构建邻接矩阵来计数包含节点对的超边数量。这种简化虽然计算方便，但会丢失信息：不同的超图可能产生相同的矩阵，且矩阵条目通常相互依赖。论文旨在研究在这种仅观测矩阵的情况下，如何有效地进行团检测与恢复。

Method: 采用谱分析方法：对于检测问题，使用谱范数检验；对于恢复问题，提出基于主特征向量的多项式时间谱方法。分析采用留一特征向量框架，并扩展到背景超边概率可能依赖于n的稀疏机制。

Result: 在√n尺度下，谱范数检验对于检测问题是渐近有效的，且性能明确依赖于背景超边概率。对于恢复问题，基于主特征向量的谱方法能够实现精确恢复，同样在√n尺度下，且性能明确依赖于背景超边概率。结果还扩展到稀疏机制。

Conclusion: 该研究为仅观测邻接矩阵的情况提供了严格的检测和恢复保证，证明了谱方法在超图数据投影到加权图后的团检测与恢复问题中的有效性，特别是在√n尺度下具有最优性能。

Abstract: Hypergraph data are often projected onto a weighted graph by constructing an adjacency matrix whose $(i,j)$ entry counts the number of hyperedges containing both nodes $i$ and $j$. This reduction is computationally convenient, but it can lose information: distinct hypergraphs may induce the same matrix, and the matrix entries are generally dependent because each hyperedge contributes to multiple pairs. We study the planted clique problem under this matrix-only observation model. For detection, we show that a spectral norm test is asymptotically powerful at the $\sqrt{n}$ scale, with explicit dependence on the background hyperedge probability. For recovery, we analyze a polynomial-time spectral method based on the leading eigenvector and prove exact recovery at the canonical $\sqrt{n}$ scale, again with explicit dependence on the background hyperedge probability. We also extend both results to sparse regimes in which the background hyperedge probability may depend on $n$. Our analysis adapts a leave-one-out eigenvector framework to this setting. These results provide rigorous detection and recovery guarantees when only the adjacency matrix is observed.

</details>


### [124] [Node-Private Community Detection in Stochastic Block Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09078)
*Olga Klopp,Ilias Zadik*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 该论文研究了随机块模型中的社区检测问题，在严格的节点级差分隐私下，发现存在固有的对数级隐私成本，这与边隐私设置不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在纯节点级差分隐私下的社区检测问题，这种隐私概念比边隐私更严格，因为单个节点的修改会影响线性多个观测值，使得问题更具挑战性。

Method: 采用基于指数机制结合扩展引理的节点隐私估计器，并建立了理论上下界分析。在算法方面分析了节点隐私估计器的性能，在理论方面建立了匹配的上下界。

Result: 在标准稀疏机制下，对数级隐私预算足以获得非平凡的恢复保证；同时证明了对数级缩放是不可避免的。在超对数隐私预算机制下，上下界给出了极小极大风险的两项匹配特征。

Conclusion: 研究识别了节点隐私社区检测中固有的对数级隐私成本（这在边差分隐私下不存在），并精确刻画了节点隐私与SBM恢复之间的权衡关系。

Abstract: We study community detection in stochastic block models under pure node-level differential privacy, a stringent notion that protects the participation of an individual together with all of their incident edges. This setting is substantially more challenging than edge-private community detection, since modifying a single node can affect linearly many observations. On the algorithmic side, we analyze a node-private estimator based on the exponential mechanism combined with an extension lemma, and show that exact recovery remains achievable. In the standard sparse regime with logarithmic average degree and a fixed number of communities, our results imply that a logarithmic privacy budget suffices to obtain nontrivial recovery guarantees. On the lower bound side, we show that this logarithmic scaling is in fact unavoidable: any pure node-private method must fail to achieve polynomially small exact-recovery error, or polynomially small expected mismatch, unless the privacy budget is at least of this order. Moreover, in the regime of super-logarithmic privacy budgets, our upper and lower bounds yield a matching two-term characterization of the minimax risk, with one term governed by the non-private statistical signal and the other by the privacy budget; these match up to universal constants in the exponents. Taken together, our results identify an inherent logarithmic privacy cost in node-private community detection, absent under edge differential privacy, and provide a precise rate-level characterization of the tradeoff between node privacy and SBM recovery.

</details>


### [125] [Testing axial symmetry around an unspecified direction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09317)
*Alejandro Cholaquidis,Juan Cuesta-Albertos,Ricardo Fraiman,Manuel Hernández-Banadik*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 提出了一种检验多元分布是否关于某个未知方向具有轴对称性的方法，通过协方差矩阵特征向量确定候选方向，使用投影数据和样本分割构建Kolmogorov-Smirnov型统计量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在统计学中，检验多元分布是否具有轴对称性是一个重要问题，但现有方法通常假设对称轴已知。实际应用中对称轴往往是未知的，需要开发能够同时检验对称性和识别对称轴的方法。

Method: 在协方差矩阵具有简单谱的假设下，对称轴必须是协方差矩阵的特征向量。方法包括：1) 确定候选方向集合（协方差矩阵的特征向量）；2) 对每个候选方向，使用投影数据和样本分割构建Kolmogorov-Smirnov型统计量；3) 推导三角阵列框架下的渐近分布；4) 在适当正则条件下建立bootstrap有效性。

Result: 建立了统计量的渐近分布理论，证明了bootstrap方法的有效性，从而为对称轴未指定的轴对称性检验提供了可行的检验程序。

Conclusion: 该方法解决了对称轴未知情况下的轴对称性检验问题，通过有限候选方向集和投影统计量，结合渐近理论和bootstrap，提供了一个完整可行的检验框架。

Abstract: We consider the problem of testing whether a multivariate distribution is axially symmetric about some unknown direction. Under a simple-spectrum assumption on the covariance matrix, any symmetry axis must coincide with an eigenvector of the covariance matrix, so the problem reduces to testing a finite set of candidate directions. For each candidate direction, we construct a Kolmogorov--Smirnov-type statistic based on projected data and sample splitting. We derive its asymptotic distribution in a triangular-array framework and establish bootstrap validity under suitable regularity conditions. This leads to a feasible testing procedure for axial symmetry when the symmetry direction is unspecified.

</details>


### [126] [Harmonic Map Regression: Rate-Optimal Nonparametric Estimation on Manifolds with Topological Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09513)
*Xiaoyu Chen*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 该论文研究了流形值响应的调和映射回归方法，通过Dirichlet能量惩罚经验Fréchet风险，建立了与经典欧氏平滑样条的平行理论，揭示了拓扑恢复现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究流形值响应的非参数回归问题，将经典的欧氏空间平滑样条理论推广到流形值数据，探索几何结构对回归估计的影响，特别是拓扑结构在回归中的作用。

Method: 提出调和映射回归方法，通过惩罚经验Fréchet风险的Dirichlet能量来构建估计器。利用调和映射理论，将无限维变分问题简化为有限维优化，建立Euler-Lagrange方程，证明解为分段测地样条，并推导等价核控制点态风险。

Result: 建立了完整的理论框架：Euler-Lagrange方程刻画解为分段测地样条；等价核控制点态风险率为n^{-2/3}；拓扑恢复现象表明在拓扑不同的回归曲线中，估计器以概率趋于1恢复正确的拓扑类；曲率相关的oracle不等式给出极小极大速率n^{-2s/(2s+1)}；五个几何障碍表明完整结构理论仅适用于Dirichlet能量(s=1)。

Conclusion: 调和映射回归为流形值响应提供了系统的非参数估计框架，建立了与欧氏平滑样条平行的理论，揭示了拓扑恢复这一流形特有的现象，在非正曲率目标上达到与欧氏空间相同的常数，但完整结构理论仅适用于Dirichlet能量惩罚。

Abstract: We study harmonic map regression, a nonparametric estimator for manifold-valued responses, that penalizes the empirical Fréchet risk by the Dirichlet energy. By connecting penalized regression to the theory of harmonic maps, the estimator acquires a structural theory that parallels the classical Euclidean smoothing spline. The Euler-Lagrange equation characterizes the solution as a piecewise-geodesic spline, an equivalent kernel controls pointwise risk at the rate $n^{-2/3}$, and the infinite-dimensional variational problem reduces exactly to a finite-dimensional optimization. Such newly established connection reveals a topological phenomenon that has no analogue in Euclidean nonparametric regression and, to our knowledge, has not been studied in the manifold regression literature. On manifolds whose regression curves can wrap around in topologically distinct ways, maps in distinct homotopy classes are separated by energy barriers intrinsic to the geometry of the target, and the Dirichlet penalty makes the estimator sensitive to this structure, recovering the correct topological class with probability tending to one, a phase transition we call topological recovery. A curvature-dependent oracle inequality yields the minimax rate $n^{-2s/(2s+1)}$ for Sobolev order $s$, matching the Euclidean constant on non-positively curved targets, while five geometric obstructions show that the full structural theory is unique to the Dirichlet energy ($s=1$). Simulations on $S^2$, $\mathbb{H}^2$, $SO(3)$, $\mathrm{Sym}^+(2)$, and $T^2$ corroborate the theory, and an application to wind-direction data on $S^1$ illustrates practical advantages.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [Hierarchical Kernel Transformer: Multi-Scale Attention with an Information-Theoretic Approximation Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08829)
*Giansalvo Cirrincione*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HKT是一种多尺度注意力机制，通过可训练因果下采样在L个分辨率级别处理序列，使用学习凸权重组合级别特定得分矩阵，计算成本仅为标准注意力的4/3倍（L=3时为1.3125倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提出一种计算效率更高的注意力机制，能够在多个尺度上捕捉序列模式，同时保持理论可解释性，解决标准注意力计算成本高和缺乏多尺度建模能力的问题。

Method: 使用可训练因果下采样在L个分辨率级别处理序列，每个级别生成特定得分矩阵，通过学习凸权重组合这些矩阵，形成分层核变换器。方法包含对称部分控制互惠注意力和反对称部分控制方向注意力的分解。

Result: 在三个数据集上均优于重新训练的标准注意力基线：合成ListOps提升4.77pp（55.10% vs 50.33%），顺序CIFAR-10提升1.44pp（35.45% vs 34.01%），IMDB字符级情感分析提升7.47pp（70.19% vs 62.72%），计算开销仅为1.31倍。

Conclusion: HKT是一种理论严谨、计算高效的多尺度注意力机制，严格包含单头标准注意力和因果卷积，提供可解释的分解和误差分析，在多个序列任务上显著优于标准注意力。

Abstract: The Hierarchical Kernel Transformer (HKT) is a multi-scale attention mechanism that processes sequences at L resolution levels via trainable causal downsampling, combining level-specific score matrices through learned convex weights. The total computational cost is bounded by 4/3 times that of standard attention, reaching 1.3125x for L = 3. Four theoretical results are established. (i) The hierarchical score matrix defines a positive semidefinite kernel under a sufficient condition on the symmetrised bilinear form (Proposition 3.1). (ii) The asymmetric score matrix decomposes uniquely into a symmetric part controlling reciprocal attention and an antisymmetric part controlling directional attention; HKT provides L independent such pairs across scales, one per resolution level (Propositions 3.5-3.6). (iii) The approximation error decomposes into three interpretable components with an explicit non-Gaussian correction and a geometric decay bound in L (Theorem 4.3, Proposition 4.4). (iv) HKT strictly subsumes single-head standard attention and causal convolution (Proposition 3.4). Experiments over 3 random seeds show consistent gains over retrained standard attention baselines: +4.77pp on synthetic ListOps (55.10+-0.29% vs 50.33+-0.12%, T = 512), +1.44pp on sequential CIFAR-10 (35.45+-0.09% vs 34.01+-0.19%, T = 1,024), and +7.47pp on IMDB character-level sentiment (70.19+-0.57% vs 62.72+-0.40%, T = 1,024), all at 1.31x overhead.

</details>


### [128] [On Divergence Measures for Training GFlowNets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.09355)
*Tiago da Silva,Eliezer de Souza da Silva,Diego Mesquita*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于不同散度最小化的GFlowNets训练方法，通过设计统计高效估计器减少梯度方差，相比传统方法显著加速收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统GFlowNets训练使用期望对数平方差最小化，虽然与变分推断相关，但直接使用标准KL散度最小化会导致有偏和高方差估计器。需要探索更有效的散度度量来改进训练。

Method: 首先回顾四种散度度量：Renyi-α、Tsallis-α、反向KL和正向KL，设计其随机梯度的统计高效估计器。使用基于REINFORCE leave-one-out和score-matching的控制变量减少学习目标梯度的方差。

Result: 适当最小化这些散度可产生理论上正确且经验有效的训练方案，通常比先前提出的优化方法收敛速度显著更快。

Conclusion: 该工作通过缩小GFlowNets训练与广义变分近似之间的差距，为基于散度最小化视角的算法思想铺平道路。

Abstract: Generative Flow Networks (GFlowNets) are amortized inference models designed to sample from unnormalized distributions over composable objects, with applications in generative modeling for tasks in fields such as causal discovery, NLP, and drug discovery. Traditionally, the training procedure for GFlowNets seeks to minimize the expected log-squared difference between a proposal (forward policy) and a target (backward policy) distribution, which enforces certain flow-matching conditions. While this training procedure is closely related to variational inference (VI), directly attempting standard Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence minimization can lead to proven biased and potentially high-variance estimators. Therefore, we first review four divergence measures, namely, Renyi-$α$'s, Tsallis-$α$'s, reverse and forward KL's, and design statistically efficient estimators for their stochastic gradients in the context of training GFlowNets. Then, we verify that properly minimizing these divergences yields a provably correct and empirically effective training scheme, often leading to significantly faster convergence than previously proposed optimization. To achieve this, we design control variates based on the REINFORCE leave-one-out and score-matching estimators to reduce the variance of the learning objectives' gradients. Our work contributes by narrowing the gap between GFlowNets training and generalized variational approximations, paving the way for algorithmic ideas informed by the divergence minimization viewpoint.

</details>


### [129] [GNN-as-Judge: Unleashing the Power of LLMs for Graph Learning with GNN Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08553)
*Ruiyao Xu,Kaize Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GNN-as-Judge框架，通过GNN的结构归纳偏置增强LLM在低资源文本属性图上的半监督学习性能，解决伪标签生成与噪声问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在文本属性图上表现优异，但在低资源场景下（标记节点严重不足）性能受限，因为微调LLM需要足够标记数据，特别是当图结构复杂时。核心挑战包括：(1) 为LLM生成和选择可靠伪标签的困难，(2) 使用伪标签微调LLM时需要减轻潜在标签噪声

Method: 提出GNN-as-Judge框架：1) 协作伪标签策略：首先识别从标记节点影响最大的未标记节点，然后利用LLM与GNN之间的一致性和分歧模式生成可靠标签；2) 弱监督LLM微调算法：从信息丰富的伪标签中蒸馏知识，同时减轻潜在标签噪声

Result: 在多个文本属性图数据集上的实验表明，GNN-as-Judge显著优于现有方法，特别是在标记数据稀缺的低资源场景下

Conclusion: 通过结合GNN的结构归纳偏置，GNN-as-Judge框架能够释放LLM在文本属性图上进行少样本半监督学习的潜力，有效解决伪标签生成和噪声问题，在低资源场景下取得显著性能提升

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown strong performance on text-attributed graphs (TAGs) due to their superior semantic understanding ability on textual node features. However, their effectiveness as predictors in the low-resource setting, where labeled nodes are severely limited and scarce, remains constrained since fine-tuning LLMs usually requires sufficient labeled data, especially when the TAG shows complex structural patterns. In essence, this paper targets two key challenges: (i) the difficulty of generating and selecting reliable pseudo labels on TAGs for LLMs, and (ii) the need to mitigate potential label noise when fine-tuning LLMs with pseudo labels. To counter the challenges, we propose a new framework, GNN-as-Judge, which can unleash the power of LLMs for few-shot semi-supervised learning on TAGs by incorporating the structural inductive bias of Graph Neural Networks (GNNs). Specifically, GNN-as-Judge introduces a collaborative pseudo-labeling strategy that first identifies the most influenced unlabeled nodes from labeled nodes, then exploits both the agreement and disagreement patterns between LLMs and GNNs to generate reliable labels. Furthermore, we develop a weakly-supervised LLM fine-tuning algorithm that can distill the knowledge from informative pseudo labels while mitigating the potential label noise. Experiments on multiple TAG datasets demonstrate that GNN-as-Judge significantly outperforms existing methods, particularly in low-resource regimes where labeled data are scarce.

</details>


### [130] [QuanBench+: A Unified Multi-Framework Benchmark for LLM-Based Quantum Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08570)
*Ali Slim,Haydar Hamieh,Jawad Kotaich,Yehya Ghosn,Mahdi Chehimi,Ammar Mohanna,Hasan Abed Al Kader Hammoud,Bernard Ghanem*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QuanBench+是一个统一的量子代码生成基准测试，跨Qiskit、PennyLane和Cirq三个框架，包含42个对齐任务，用于评估大语言模型在量子算法、门分解和状态准备方面的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前量子代码生成评估大多局限于单一框架，难以区分量子推理能力与框架熟悉度，需要统一的跨框架基准来更准确地评估大语言模型的量子代码生成能力。

Method: 构建QuanBench+基准测试，包含42个对齐任务覆盖量子算法、门分解和状态准备；使用可执行功能测试评估模型，报告Pass@1和Pass@5；采用基于KL散度的概率输出接受标准；研究基于反馈的修复机制，允许模型在运行时错误或错误答案后修订代码。

Result: 最佳单次生成得分：Qiskit 59.5%、Cirq 54.8%、PennyLane 42.9%；基于反馈修复后最佳得分：Qiskit 83.3%、Cirq 76.2%、PennyLane 66.7%。结果表明量子代码生成有明显进展，但可靠的多框架量子代码生成仍未解决，仍高度依赖框架特定知识。

Conclusion: 量子代码生成在多框架环境中仍面临挑战，需要进一步研究以降低对框架特定知识的依赖，提高模型的量子推理能力和跨框架适应性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used for code generation, yet quantum code generation is still evaluated mostly within single frameworks, making it difficult to separate quantum reasoning from framework familiarity. We introduce QuanBench+, a unified benchmark spanning Qiskit, PennyLane, and Cirq, with 42 aligned tasks covering quantum algorithms, gate decomposition, and state preparation.
  We evaluate models with executable functional tests, report Pass@1 and Pass@5, and use KL-divergence-based acceptance for probabilistic outputs. We additionally study Pass@1 after feedback-based repair, where a model may revise code after a runtime error or wrong answer. Across frameworks, the strongest one-shot scores reach 59.5% in Qiskit, 54.8% in Cirq, and 42.9% in PennyLane; with feedback-based repair, the best scores rise to 83.3%, 76.2%, and 66.7%, respectively. These results show clear progress, but also that reliable multi-framework quantum code generation remains unsolved and still depends strongly on framework-specific knowledge.

</details>


### [131] [Robust Reasoning Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08571)
*Pavel Golikov,Evgenii Opryshko,Gennady Pekhimenko,Mark C. Jeffrey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出14种扰动技术评估LLM数学推理鲁棒性，发现开源模型在扰动下性能大幅下降，且标准注意力机制会被中间推理步骤污染导致后续问题准确率衰减


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型在标准数学基准测试中表现优异，但其推理过程严重过拟合于标准文本格式，缺乏对格式扰动的鲁棒性。需要系统评估LLM推理的脆弱性并理解其根本原因。

Method: 提出包含14种扰动技术的评估管道，应用于AIME 2024数据集，评估8个SOTA模型。为分离机械解析失败与下游推理失败，设计实验强制模型在单个上下文窗口中顺序解决多个未扰动数学问题，以隔离工作记忆容量。

Result: 前沿模型展现韧性，但开源推理模型在扰动下遭受灾难性崩溃（平均准确率下降高达55%，某些扰动下100%）。7B到120B参数的开源模型及Claude Opus 4.6在后续问题上显示准确率衰减，表明中间推理步骤永久污染了标准密集注意力机制。

Conclusion: 为实现可靠推理，未来推理架构必须在模型自身的思维链中集成显式上下文重置，这引出了关于原子推理任务最优粒度的根本性开放问题。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) achieve high performance on standard mathematical benchmarks, their underlying reasoning processes remain highly overfit to standard textual formatting. We propose a perturbation pipeline consisting of 14 techniques to evaluate robustness of LLM reasoning. We apply this pipeline to AIME 2024 dataset and evalute 8 state-of-the-art models on the resulting benchmark. While frontier models exhibit resilience, open weights reasoning models suffer catastrophic collapses (up to 55% average accuracy drops across perturbations and up to 100% on some), exposing structural fragility. To further disentangle mechanical parsing failures from downstream reasoning failures, we strictly isolate the models' working memory capacity by forcing models to solve multiple unperturbed mathematical problems sequentially within a single context window. Our results indicate that open weight models ranging from 7B to 120B parameters and Claude Opus 4.6 exhibit accuracy decay on subsequent problems. This degradation demonstrates that intermediate reasoning steps permanently pollute standard dense attention mechanisms. We argue that to achieve reliable reasoning, future reasoning architectures must integrate explicit contextual resets within a model's own Chain-of-Thought, leading to fundamental open questions regarding the optimal granularity of atomic reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [132] [Distilling Genomic Models for Efficient mRNA Representation Learning via Embedding Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08574)
*Rasched Haidari,Sam Martin,Maxime Allard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种用于mRNA序列的蒸馏框架，将大型基因组基础模型的知识转移到小200倍的专用模型中，在计算受限时实现高效推理


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型基因组基础模型虽然性能优异但参数量巨大（数十亿参数），计算成本高昂，在计算资源有限时难以部署使用

Method: 采用嵌入层蒸馏框架，将最先进基因组基础模型的mRNA表示转移到专用小模型中，相比基于logit的蒸馏方法更稳定有效

Result: 在mRNA-bench基准测试中，蒸馏模型在同等规模模型中达到最先进性能，并能与更大架构竞争mRNA相关任务

Conclusion: 基于嵌入的mRNA序列蒸馏是生物基础模型的有效训练策略，为计算受限场景下的基因组高效可扩展序列建模提供了可行方案

Abstract: Large Genomic Foundation Models have recently achieved remarkable results and in-vivo translation capabilities. However these models quickly grow to over a few Billion of parameters and are expensive to run when compute is limited. To overcome this challenge, we present a distillation framework for transferring mRNA representations from a state of the art genomic foundation model into a much smaller model specialized for mRNA sequences, reducing the size by 200-fold. Embedding-level distillation worked better than logit based methods, which we found unstable. Benchmarking on mRNA-bench demonstrates that the distilled model achieves state-of-the-art performance among models of comparable size and competes with larger architectures for mRNA-related tasks. Our results highlight embedding-based distillation of mRNA sequences as an effective training strategy for biological foundation models. This enables similar efficient and scalable sequence modelling in genomics, particularly when large models are computationally challenging or infeasible.

</details>


### [133] [MolPaQ: Modular Quantum-Classical Patch Learning for Interpretable Molecular Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08575)
*Syed Rameez Naqvi,Lu Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MOLPAQ：一种模块化量子-经典分子生成器，通过量子生成的潜在片段组装分子，实现100%有效性、99.75%新颖性和0.905多样性，量子生成器在性质控制方面优于参数匹配的经典生成器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分子生成模型通常在有效性、多样性和性质控制之间进行权衡，无法同时保证这三个关键目标。需要开发一种能够兼顾这些目标的分子生成方法。

Method: MOLPAQ采用模块化架构：1）β-VAE在QM9数据集上预训练学习化学对齐的潜在流形；2）降维条件器将分子描述符映射到该空间；3）参数高效的量子片段生成器产生纠缠节点嵌入；4）价态感知聚合器重建为有效分子图；5）使用潜在批评器和化学形状奖励进行对抗性微调。

Result: 实现100% RDKit有效性、99.75%新颖性和0.905多样性。量子生成器相比参数匹配的经典生成器，平均QED提高约2.3%，芳香基团出现率增加约10-12%，表明其作为紧凑拓扑塑造算子的作用。

Conclusion: MOLPAQ通过量子-经典混合架构成功解决了分子生成中有效性、多样性和性质控制的权衡问题，量子生成器在性质优化方面展现出独特优势，为分子设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: Molecular generative models must jointly ensure validity, diversity, and property control, yet existing approaches typically trade off among these objectives. We present MOLPAQ, a modular quantum-classical generator that assembles molecules from quantum-generated latent patches. A \b{eta}-VAE pretrained on QM9 learns a chemically aligned latent manifold; a reduced conditioner maps molecular descriptors into this space; and a parameter-efficient quantum patch generator produces entangled node embeddings that a valence-aware aggregator reconstructs into valid molecular graphs. Adversarial fine-tuning with a latent critic and chemistry-shaped reward yields 100\% RDKit validity, 99.75\% novelty, and 0.905 diversity. Beyond aggregate metrics, the pretrained quantum generator, steered by the conditioner, improves mean QED by approx. 2.3\% and increases aromatic motif incidence by approx. 10-12\% relative to a parameter-matched classical generator, highlighting its role as a compact topology-shaping operator.

</details>


### [134] [Structured Exploration and Exploitation of Label Functions for Automated Data Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08578)
*Phong Lam,Ha-Linh Nguyen,Thu-Trang Nguyen,Son Nguyen,Hieu Dinh Vo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EXPONA：一种自动程序化标注框架，通过多级标签函数探索和可靠性感知过滤，在多样性和可靠性之间取得平衡，显著提升弱标签质量和下游性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量标注数据对机器学习模型训练至关重要，但手动标注成本高且易出错。现有自动标签函数生成方法要么依赖大语言模型生成表面启发式规则，要么基于手工原语进行模型合成，导致覆盖范围有限和标签质量不可靠。

Method: EXPONA将标签函数生成构建为平衡多样性和可靠性的原则性过程，系统探索表面、结构和语义三个层次的多级标签函数，并应用可靠性感知机制抑制噪声或冗余启发式规则，同时保留互补信号。

Result: 在11个跨领域分类数据集上的实验表明，EXPONA始终优于最先进的自动标签函数生成方法：达到近完全标签覆盖（最高98.9%），弱标签质量提升最高87%，下游加权F1性能提升最高46%。

Conclusion: EXPONA通过多级标签函数探索和可靠性感知过滤的组合，能够在生成的标签函数集中平衡覆盖率和精度，从而在不同任务中实现更一致的标签质量和下游性能。

Abstract: High-quality labeled data is critical for training reliable machine learning and deep learning models, yet manual annotation remains costly and error-prone. Programmatic labeling addresses this challenge by using label functions (LFs), i.e., heuristic rules that automatically generate weak labels for training datasets. However, existing automated LF generation methods either rely on large language models (LLMs) to synthesize surface-level heuristics or employ model-based synthesis over hand-crafted primitives. These approaches often result in limited coverage and unreliable label quality. In this paper, we introduce EXPONA, an automated framework for programmatic labeling that formulates LF generation as a principled process balancing diversity and reliability. EXPONA systematically explores multi-level LFs, spanning surface, structural, and semantic perspectives. EXPONA further applies reliability-aware mechanisms to suppress noisy or redundant heuristics while preserving complementary signals. To evaluate EXPONA, we conducted extensive experiments on eleven classification datasets across diverse domains. Experimental results show that EXPONA consistently outperformed state-of-the-art automated LF generation methods. Specifically, EXPONA achieved nearly complete label coverage (up to 98.9%), improved weak label quality by up to 87%, and yielded downstream performance gains of up to 46% in weighted F1. These results indicate that EXPONA's combination of multi-level LF exploration and reliability-aware filtering enabled more consistent label quality and downstream performance across diverse tasks by balancing coverage and precision in the generated LF set.

</details>


### [135] [On the Spectral Geometry of Cross-Modal Representations: A Functional Map Diagnostic for Multimodal Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08579)
*Krisanu Sarkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 使用计算几何中的函数映射框架研究独立预训练视觉和语言编码器之间的跨模态对齐，发现模型在捕获的结构量上收敛但组织方式不同，存在谱复杂度-方向间隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究独立预训练的视觉（DINOv2）和语言（all-MiniLM-L6-v2）编码器之间的跨模态对齐，探索表示流形之间的对应关系，特别是使用函数映射框架来分析表示的结构特性。

Method: 使用计算几何中的函数映射框架，将表示流形之间的对应关系表示为图拉普拉斯特征基之间的紧凑线性算子。该方法与Procrustes对齐和相对表示方法进行比较，并分析拉普拉斯特征值谱和特征向量基的对齐情况。

Result: 函数映射框架在所有监督预算下的跨模态检索性能均不如Procrustes对齐和相对表示。然而发现两个编码器的拉普拉斯特征值谱定量相似（归一化谱距离0.043），表明独立训练的模型发展出具有可比内在复杂度的流形。但函数映射显示接近零的对角优势（均值低于0.05）和大的正交性误差（70.15），表明特征向量基基本未对齐。

Conclusion: 发现了谱复杂度-方向间隙：模型在捕获多少结构上收敛，但在如何组织这些结构上不收敛。这一间隙定义了谱对齐方法的边界条件，并提出了三个诊断量：对角优势、正交性偏差和拉普拉斯交换性误差，用于表征跨模态表示的兼容性。

Abstract: We study cross-modal alignment between independently pretrained vision (DINOv2) and language (all-MiniLM-L6-v2) encoders using the functional map framework from computational geometry, which represents correspondence between representation manifolds as a compact linear operator between graph Laplacian eigenbases. While the framework underperforms Procrustes alignment and relative representations for cross-modal retrieval across all supervision budgets, it reveals a structural property of multimodal representations. We find that the Laplacian eigenvalue spectra of the two encoders are quantitatively similar (normalized spectral distance 0.043), indicating that independently trained models develop manifolds of comparable intrinsic complexity. However, the functional map exhibits near-zero diagonal dominance (mean below 0.05) and large orthogonality error (70.15), showing that the eigenvector bases are effectively unaligned. We term this decoupling the spectral complexity--orientation gap: models converge in how much structure they capture but not in how they organize it. This gap defines a boundary condition for spectral alignment methods and motivates three diagnostic quantities : diagonal dominance, orthogonality deviation, and Laplacian commutativity error for characterizing cross-modal representation compatibility.

</details>


### [136] [Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection via Dual-Branch Reconstruction and Autoregressive Flow-based Residual Density Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08582)
*Jun Liu,Ying Chen,Ziqian Lu,Qinyue Tong,Jun Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DBR-AF是一个用于多元时间序列异常检测的新框架，通过双分支重建编码器和自回归流模块解决现有方法中的过拟合和误导性异常评分问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于重建的异常检测方法存在两个关键限制：1) 过度强调跨变量建模导致虚假相关性的过拟合；2) 简单累加多变量重建误差会产生误导性异常评分，难以区分难以重建的样本和真实异常。

Method: 提出DBR-AF框架，包含双分支重建编码器（DBR）和自回归流模块（AF）。DBR编码器解耦跨变量相关性学习和变量内统计特性建模以缓解虚假相关性；AF模块使用多个堆叠的可逆变换来建模复杂的多元残差分布，并利用密度估计准确识别具有大重建误差的正常样本。

Result: 在七个基准数据集上的广泛实验表明，DBR-AF实现了最先进的性能，消融研究验证了其核心组件的不可或缺性。

Conclusion: DBR-AF通过解耦跨变量和变量内建模以及使用自回归流进行密度估计，有效解决了现有重建方法在多元时间序列异常检测中的局限性，取得了优异的检测性能。

Abstract: Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection (MTSAD) is critical for real-world monitoring scenarios such as industrial control and aerospace systems. Mainstream reconstruction-based anomaly detection methods suffer from two key limitations: first, overfitting to spurious correlations induced by an overemphasis on cross-variable modeling; second, the generation of misleading anomaly scores by simply summing up multivariable reconstruction errors, which makes it difficult to distinguish between hard-to-reconstruct samples and genuine anomalies. To address these issues, we propose DBR-AF, a novel framework that integrates a dual-branch reconstruction (DBR) encoder and an autoregressive flow (AF) module. The DBR encoder decouples cross-variable correlation learning and intra-variable statistical property modeling to mitigate spurious correlations, while the AF module employs multiple stacked reversible transformations to model the complex multivariate residual distribution and further leverages density estimation to accurately identify normal samples with large reconstruction errors. Extensive experiments on seven benchmark datasets demonstrate that DBR-AF achieves state-of-the-art performance, with ablation studies validating the indispensability of its core components.

</details>


### [137] [CSAttention: Centroid-Scoring Attention for Accelerating LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08584)
*Chuxu Song,Zhencan Peng,Jiuqi Wei,Chuanhui Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CSAttention是一种面向可重用上下文的高吞吐量稀疏注意力方法，通过离线预填充阶段构建查询中心查找表，在线解码时用高效表查找替代全上下文扫描，在95%稀疏度和32K-128K长上下文下实现接近全注意力的精度和4.6倍推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文LLM中，可重用预填充提示导致注意力计算和KV缓存成为解码时的主要瓶颈。现有稀疏注意力方法在高稀疏度下难以保持精度，因为查询和键之间存在固有的分布偏移。

Method: CSAttention采用存储换计算的策略，针对离线预填充/在线解码场景优化：1）离线预填充阶段构建查询中心查找表，其大小在解码期间固定；2）在线解码时用高效表查找和GPU友好的分数累加替代全上下文扫描。

Result: 在95%高稀疏度和32K-128K长上下文设置下，CSAttention实现了接近全注意力的精度，在模型精度和推理速度上均优于最先进的稀疏注意力方法，在128K上下文长度下比最准确的基线实现4.6倍推理加速。

Conclusion: CSAttention是一种有效的训练无关稀疏注意力方法，特别适用于可重用上下文的高吞吐量服务场景，通过离线预填充阶段的前期计算摊销，显著优化了每步解码延迟。

Abstract: Long-context LLMs increasingly rely on extended, reusable prefill prompts for agents and domain Q&A, pushing attention and KV-cache to become the dominant decode-time bottlenecks. While sparse attention reduces computation and transfer costs, it often struggles to maintain accuracy at high sparsity levels due to the inherent distribution shift between Queries and Keys. We propose Centroid-Scoring Attention (CSAttention), a training-free sparse attention method optimized for high-throughput serving of reusable contexts. CSAttention adopts a storage-for-computation strategy tailored to the offline-prefill/online-decode setting: it front-loads computation into a one-time offline prefill phase that can be amortized across multiple queries, while aggressively optimizing per-step decoding latency. Specifically, CSAttention constructs query-centric lookup tables during offline prefill, whose size remains fixed during decoding, and enables online decoding to replace full-context scans with efficient table lookups and GPU-friendly score accumulation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CSAttention achieves near-identical accuracy to full attention. Under high sparsity (95%) and long-context settings (32K-128K), CSAttention consistently outperforms state-of-the-art sparse attention methods in both model accuracy and inference speed, achieving up to 4.6x inference speedup over the most accurate baseline at a context length of 128K.

</details>


### [138] [FluidFlow: a flow-matching generative model for fluid dynamics surrogates on unstructured meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08586)
*David Ramos,Lucas Lacasa,Fermín Gutiérrez,Eusebio Valero,Gonzalo Rubio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于条件流匹配的生成模型FluidFlow，用于构建可扩展的流体动力学替代模型，可直接处理结构化和非结构化网格数据，无需插值预处理


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算流体动力学(CFD)模拟计算成本高，传统深度学习替代模型存在局限性，需要一种能够直接处理复杂网格数据、保持几何保真度的新型生成建模框架

Method: 基于条件流匹配的生成模型FluidFlow，使用U-Net和扩散变换器(DiT)两种核心架构，直接处理CFD数据，无需网格插值预处理，保持几何保真度

Result: 在两个基准问题上表现优异：翼型边界压力系数预测和三维飞机几何体压力/摩擦系数预测，均显著优于多层感知机基线，误差更低，泛化能力更强；基于变换器的架构在大型非结构化数据集上实现可扩展学习

Conclusion: 流匹配生成模型为流体动力学替代建模提供了有效灵活框架，具有实际工程和科学应用潜力，特别适用于处理复杂网格数据

Abstract: Computational fluid dynamics (CFD) provides high-fidelity simulations of fluid flows but remains computationally expensive for many-query applications. In recent years deep learning (DL) has been used to construct data-driven fluid-dynamic surrogate models. In this work we consider a different learning paradigm and embrace generative modelling as a framework for constructing scalable fluid-dynamics surrogate models. We introduce FluidFlow, a generative model based on conditional flow-matching, a recent alternative to diffusion models that learns deterministic transport maps between noise and data distributions. FluidFlow is specifically designed to operate directly on CFD data defined on both structured and unstructured meshes alike, without the needs to perform any mesh interpolation pre-processing and preserving geometric fidelity. We assess the capabilities of FluidFlow using two different core neural network architectures, a U-Net and diffusion transformer (DiT), and condition their learning on physically meaningful parameters. The methodology is validated on two benchmark problems of increasing complexity: prediction of pressure coefficients along an airfoil boundary across different operating conditions, and prediction of pressure and friction coefficients over a full three-dimensional aircraft geometry discretized on a large unstructured mesh. In both cases, FluidFlow outperform strong multilayer perceptron baselines, achieving significantly lower error metrics and improved generalisation across operating conditions. Notably, the transformer-based architecture enables scalable learning on large unstructured datasets while maintaining high predictive accuracy. These results demonstrate that flow-matching generative models provide an effective and flexible framework for surrogate modelling in fluid dynamics, with potential for realistic engineering and scientific applications.

</details>


### [139] [Act or Escalate? Evaluating Escalation Behavior in Automation with Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08588)
*Matthew DosSantos DiSorbo,Harang Ju*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究了LLM在不确定性下的决策行为，重点关注何时行动与何时升级的权衡，发现模型间存在显著差异且自我估计存在校准问题，通过干预实验证明基于思维链的监督微调能产生最稳健的决策策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有效自动化的核心在于决定何时自主行动、何时需要人工干预。当前LLM在不确定性下的决策行为缺乏系统研究，特别是模型如何权衡行动与升级的成本，以及不同模型间的决策阈值差异。

Method: 建立不确定性下的决策框架：LLM形成预测→估计正确概率→比较行动与升级的期望成本。在五个领域（需求预测、内容推荐、内容审核、贷款审批、自动驾驶）测试多种模型家族，分析其隐式决策阈值。测试三种干预：调整成本比率、提供准确性信号、训练模型遵循期望的升级规则。

Result: 发现模型间决策阈值存在显著差异，且不受架构或规模预测；模型自我估计存在模型特定的校准错误。提示工程主要对推理模型有效；基于思维链目标的监督微调产生最稳健的策略，能够泛化到不同数据集、成本比率、提示框架和未见领域。

Conclusion: 升级行为是模型特定的属性，部署前需要系统表征；通过训练模型显式推理不确定性和决策成本，可以实现更稳健的对齐。基于思维链的监督微调是获得泛化决策策略的有效方法。

Abstract: Effective automation hinges on deciding when to act and when to escalate. We model this as a decision under uncertainty: an LLM forms a prediction, estimates its probability of being correct, and compares the expected costs of acting and escalating. Using this framework across five domains of recorded human decisions-demand forecasting, content recommendation, content moderation, loan approval, and autonomous driving-and across multiple model families, we find marked differences in the implicit thresholds models use to trade off these costs. These thresholds vary substantially and are not predicted by architecture or scale, while self-estimates are miscalibrated in model-specific ways. We then test interventions that target this decision process by varying cost ratios, providing accuracy signals, and training models to follow the desired escalation rule. Prompting helps mainly for reasoning models. SFT on chain-of-thought targets yields the most robust policies, which generalize across datasets, cost ratios, prompt framings, and held-out domains. These results suggest that escalation behavior is a model-specific property that should be characterized before deployment, and that robust alignment benefits from training models to reason explicitly about uncertainty and decision costs.

</details>


### [140] [Memory-Guided Trust-Region Bayesian Optimization (MG-TuRBO) for High Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08569)
*Abhilasha Saroj,Shaked Regev,Guanhao Xu,Jinghui Yuan,Roy Luo,Ross Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 比较遗传算法与多种贝叶斯优化方法在交通仿真标定问题中的性能，提出记忆引导TuRBO方法在高维问题中表现优异


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通仿真和数字孪生标定是一个具有有限仿真预算的挑战性优化问题。每次试验都需要昂贵的仿真运行，且标定输入与模型误差之间的关系通常是非凸且带有噪声的。随着标定参数数量的增加，问题变得更加困难。

Method: 比较遗传算法(GA)与多种贝叶斯优化方法(BOMs)：经典贝叶斯优化(BO)、Trust-Region BO (TuRBO)、Multi-TuRBO以及提出的记忆引导TuRBO (MG-TuRBO)方法。在2个真实世界交通仿真标定问题(14D和84D)上评估性能，研究两种获取策略：Thompson采样和新的自适应策略。

Result: 贝叶斯优化方法在低维问题中比遗传算法更快达到良好标定目标。MG-TuRBO在14D问题中表现相当，在84D高维问题中表现出明显优势，特别是与自适应策略结合时。

Conclusion: MG-TuRBO特别适用于高维交通仿真标定问题，可能也适用于一般高维优化问题。贝叶斯优化方法在有限仿真预算下比遗传算法更有效。

Abstract: Traffic simulation and digital-twin calibration is a challenging optimization problem with a limited simulation budget. Each trial requires an expensive simulation run, and the relationship between calibration inputs and model error is often nonconvex, and noisy. The problem becomes more difficult as the number of calibration parameters increases. We compare a commonly used automatic calibration method, a genetic algorithm (GA), with Bayesian optimization methods (BOMs): classical Bayesian optimization (BO), Trust-Region BO (TuRBO), Multi-TuRBO, and a proposed Memory-Guided TuRBO (MG-TuRBO) method. We compare performance on 2 real-world traffic simulation calibration problems with 14 and 84 decision variables, representing lower- and higher-dimensional (14D and 84D) settings. For BOMs, we study two acquisition strategies, Thompson sampling and a novel adaptive strategy. We evaluate performance using final calibration quality, convergence behavior, and consistency across runs. The results show that BOMs reach good calibration targets much faster than GA in the lower-D problem. MG-TuRBO performs comparably in our 14D setting, it demonstrates noticeable advantages in the 84D problem, particularly when paired with our adaptive strategy. Our results suggest that MG-TuRBO is especially useful for high-D traffic simulation calibration and potentially for high-D problems in general.

</details>


### [141] [From Selection to Scheduling: Federated Geometry-Aware Correction Makes Exemplar Replay Work Better under Continual Dynamic Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08617)
*Zhuang Qi,Ying-Peng Tang,Lei Meng,Guoqing Chao,Lei Wu,Han Yu,Xiangxu Meng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FEAT：一种联邦几何感知校正方法，通过几何结构对齐和基于能量的几何校正模块，缓解联邦持续学习中类别不平衡导致的表示崩溃问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦持续学习中的示例回放方法主要关注样本重要性估计，但忽略了如何有效利用所选示例，这限制了在客户端和任务间持续动态异质性下的性能。特别需要解决类别不平衡导致的表示崩溃问题，即稀有类别特征被拖向频繁类别。

Method: 提出FEAT方法，包含两个核心模块：1) 几何结构对齐模块：通过对齐特征表示与其对应等角紧框架原型之间的成对角度相似性，执行结构知识蒸馏，确保跨任务的几何一致性；2) 基于能量的几何校正模块：从特征嵌入中移除任务无关的方向分量，减少对多数类别的预测偏差。

Result: 该方法缓解了不平衡导致的表示崩溃，提高了对少数类别的敏感性，增强了模型在类别不平衡分布下的鲁棒性，并减轻了表示漂移问题。

Conclusion: FEAT通过几何感知的校正机制，有效解决了联邦持续学习中示例回放方法的局限性，在跨客户端和任务的持续动态异质性下表现出更好的性能。

Abstract: Exemplar replay has become an effective strategy for mitigating catastrophic forgetting in federated continual learning (FCL) by retaining representative samples from past tasks. Existing studies focus on designing sample-importance estimation mechanisms to identify information-rich samples. However, they typically overlook strategies for effectively utilizing the selected exemplars, which limits their performance under continual dynamic heterogeneity across clients and tasks. To address this issue, this paper proposes a Federated gEometry-Aware correcTion method, termed FEAT, which alleviates imbalance-induced representation collapse that drags rare-class features toward frequent classes across clients. Specifically, it consists of two key modules: 1) the Geometric Structure Alignment module performs structural knowledge distillation by aligning the pairwise angular similarities between feature representations and their corresponding Equiangular Tight Frame prototypes, which are fixed and shared across clients to serve as a class-discriminative reference structure. This encourages geometric consistency across tasks and helps mitigate representation drift; 2) the Energy-based Geometric Correction module removes task-irrelevant directional components from feature embeddings, which reduces prediction bias toward majority classes. This improves sensitivity to minority classes and enhances the model's robustness under class-imbalanced distributions.

</details>


### [142] [Ranked Activation Shift for Post-Hoc Out-of-Distribution Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08572)
*Gianluca Guglielmo,Marc Masana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需超参数调优的后验OOD检测方法，通过固定参考分布替换排序激活幅度，解决了现有方法在不同数据集和模型上性能不一致的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于中间层激活编辑的后验OOD检测方法在不同数据集和模型上表现不稳定。研究发现这种不稳定性源于激活分布的差异，并识别了基于缩放方法在末层激活未修正时的失效模式。

Method: 提出了一种无需超参数的后验方法，用固定的分布内参考分布替换排序的激活幅度。该方法为即插即用方案，不假设末层激活函数类型，无需超参数调优，且通过构造保持分布内分类精度。

Result: 该方法在不同数据集和架构上表现出强大且一致的性能，同时保持分布内分类精度。分析表明，抑制和激发激活偏移都独立地贡献于更好的OOD区分能力。

Conclusion: 通过分析激活分布差异导致的现有方法不稳定性，提出了一种简单有效的超参数无关OOD检测方法，解决了跨数据集和模型的性能一致性问题。

Abstract: State-of-the-art post-hoc out-of-distribution detection methods rely on intermediate layer activation editing. However, they exhibit inconsistent performance across datasets and models. We show that this instability is driven by differences in the activation distributions, and identify a failure mode of scaling-based methods that arises when penultimate layer activations are not rectified. Motivated by this analysis, we propose \ours, a hyperparameter-free post-hoc method that replaces sorted activation magnitudes with a fixed in-distribution reference profile. Our simple plug-and-play method shows strong and consistent performance across datasets and architectures without assumptions on the penultimate layer activation function, and without requiring any hyperparameter tuning, while preserving in-distribution classification accuracy by construction. We further analyze what drives the improvement, showing that both inhibiting and exciting activation shifts independently contribute to better out-of-distribution discrimination.

</details>


### [143] [StructRL: Recovering Dynamic Programming Structure from Learning Dynamics in Distributional Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08620)
*Ivo Nowak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过分析分布式强化学习的学习动态，论文发现可以从学习过程中恢复动态规划式的结构信息，并提出StructRL框架利用这些信号指导采样


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习被视为均匀的数据驱动优化过程，缺乏对全局结构的显式利用，而动态规划方法依赖结构化信息传播实现高效稳定学习。本文旨在探索是否可以从分布式强化学习的学习动态中恢复这种结构

Method: 分析回报分布的时间演化，识别状态空间中学习发生的信号；引入时间学习指标t*(s)反映状态在训练期间经历最强学习更新的时刻；基于此提出StructRL框架，利用这些信号指导采样以对齐新兴的传播结构

Result: 经验证据表明时间学习指标t*(s)诱导的状态排序与动态规划式的信息传播一致；初步结果显示分布式学习动态提供了恢复和利用动态规划式结构的机制，无需显式模型

Conclusion: 分布式学习动态为强化学习提供了新的视角：学习可解释为结构化传播过程而非纯粹均匀优化过程，这为无需显式模型即可利用动态规划式结构提供了新途径

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is typically treated as a uniform, data-driven optimization process, where updates are guided by rewards and temporal-difference errors without explicitly exploiting global structure. In contrast, dynamic programming methods rely on structured information propagation, enabling efficient and stable learning. In this paper, we provide evidence that such structure can be recovered from the learning dynamics of distributional reinforcement learning. By analyzing the temporal evolution of return distributions, we identify signals that capture when and where learning occurs in the state space. In particular, we introduce a temporal learning indicator t*(s) that reflects when a state undergoes its strongest learning update during training. Empirically, this signal induces an ordering over states that is consistent with a dynamic programming-style propagation of information. Building on this observation, we propose StructRL, a framework that exploits these signals to guide sampling in alignment with the emerging propagation structure. Our preliminary results suggest that distributional learning dynamics provide a mechanism to recover and exploit dynamic programming-like structure without requiring an explicit model. This offers a new perspective on reinforcement learning, where learning can be interpreted as a structured propagation process rather than a purely uniform optimization procedure.

</details>


### [144] [VOLTA: The Surprising Ineffectiveness of Auxiliary Losses for Calibrated Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08639)
*Rahul D Ray,Utkarsh Srivastava*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VOLTA：一种轻量级确定性不确定性量化方法，在准确率、校准误差和OOD检测方面优于10种主流基线方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在安全关键应用中部署深度学习模型需要可靠的不确定性量化，但目前缺乏对各种UQ方法在不同数据模态和分布偏移下性能的共识评估

Method: 提出简化的VOLTA变体：仅包含深度编码器、可学习原型、交叉熵损失和后处理温度缩放；在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100、SVHN、均匀噪声、CIFAR-10-C和Tiny ImageNet特征上系统评估10种UQ基线方法

Result: VOLTA在CIFAR-10上达到0.864准确率，校准误差显著降低（0.010 vs. 基线0.044-0.102），OOD检测AUROC为0.802；统计测试表明VOLTA匹配或优于大多数基线

Conclusion: VOLTA作为轻量级、确定性且校准良好的UQ方法，为复杂UQ方法提供了有竞争力的替代方案，消融研究证实自适应温度和深度编码器的重要性

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification (UQ) is essential for deploying deep learning models in safety critical applications, yet no consensus exists on which UQ method performs best across different data modalities and distribution shifts. This paper presents a comprehensive benchmark of ten widely used UQ baselines including MC Dropout, SWAG, ensemble methods, temperature scaling, energy based OOD, Mahalanobis, hyperbolic classifiers, ENN, Taylor Sensus, and split conformal prediction against a simplified yet highly effective variant of VOLTA that retains only a deep encoder, learnable prototypes, cross entropy loss, and post hoc temperature scaling. We evaluate all methods on CIFAR 10 (in distribution), CIFAR 100, SVHN, uniform noise (out of distribution), CIFAR 10 C (corruptions), and Tiny ImageNet features (tabular). VOLTA achieves competitive or superior accuracy (up to 0.864 on CIFAR 10), significantly lower expected calibration error (0.010 vs. 0.044 to 0.102 for baselines), and strong OOD detection (AUROC 0.802). Statistical testing over three random seeds shows that VOLTA matches or outperforms most baselines, with ablation studies confirming the importance of adaptive temperature and deep encoders. Our results establish VOLTA as a lightweight, deterministic, and well calibrated alternative to more complex UQ approaches.

</details>


### [145] [Fully Autonomous Z-Score-Based TinyML Anomaly Detection on Resource-Constrained MCUs Using Power Side-Channel Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08581)
*Abdulrahman Albaiz,Fathi Amsaad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种完全自主的TinyML异常检测系统，基于Z-Score算法部署在低功耗微控制器上，通过电源侧信道数据实时监测电器行为，无需离线训练或云端辅助。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物联网异常检测方法通常依赖离线训练或云端分析，这限制了在资源受限的微控制器上的自主部署。需要一种完全在设备上完成训练和推理的轻量级解决方案。

Method: 系统在STM32平台上实现，持续采样电流消耗，在设备上计算RMS值，在初始训练阶段推导统计参数。使用轻量级Z-Score阈值进行异常检测，实现可解释且计算高效的推理。

Result: 在14天的家用迷你冰箱数据集上评估，实现了完美的检测性能（精确度和召回率均为1.00），推理延迟在数十微秒级别，总内存占用约3.3KB SRAM和63KB Flash。

Conclusion: 验证了在低成本微控制器上实现稳健且完全自主的TinyML异常检测的可行性。未来工作包括扩展框架以纳入更多轻量级模型和多设备学习场景。

Abstract: This paper presents a fully autonomous Tiny Machine Learning (TinyML) Z-Score-based anomaly detection system deployed on a low-power microcontroller for real-time monitoring of appliance behavior using power side-channel data. Unlike existing Internet of Things (IoT) anomaly detection approaches that rely on offline training or cloud-assisted analytics, the proposed system performs both model training and inference directly on a resource-constrained microcontroller without external computation or connectivity. The system continuously samples current consumption, computes Root Mean Square (RMS) values on-device, and derives statistical parameters during an initial training phase. Anomalies are detected using lightweight Z-Score thresholds, enabling interpretable and computationally efficient inference suitable for embedded deployment. The architecture was implemented on an STM32-based platform and evaluated using a 14-day dataset collected from a household mini-fridge under normal operation and controlled anomaly conditions. Results demonstrate perfect detection performance, with Precision and Recall of 1.00, inference latencies on the order of tens of microseconds, and a total memory footprint of approximately 3.3 KB SRAM and 63 KB Flash. These results confirm that robust and fully autonomous TinyML anomaly detection can be achieved on low-cost microcontrollers. Future work includes extending the framework to incorporate additional lightweight models and multi-device learning scenarios.

</details>


### [146] [Every Response Counts: Quantifying Uncertainty of LLM-based Multi-Agent Systems through Tensor Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08708)
*Tiejin Chen,Huaiyuan Yao,Jia Chen,Evangelos E. Papalexakis,Hua Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MATU：通过张量分解量化多智能体系统中不确定性的新框架，解决传统方法无法处理的级联不确定性、通信路径可变性和拓扑多样性挑战


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型多智能体系统在复杂任务上优于单智能体系统，但其复杂的交互引入了通信动态和角色依赖带来的可靠性挑战。现有不确定性量化方法通常为单轮输出设计，无法处理MAS特有的复杂性，特别是级联不确定性、通信路径可变性和通信拓扑多样性三个挑战。

Method: 提出MATU框架，通过张量分解量化不确定性。该方法不仅分析最终文本输出，而是将整个推理轨迹表示为嵌入矩阵，并将多次执行运行组织成高阶张量。通过张量分解，分离并量化不同的不确定性来源，提供可推广到不同智能体结构的全面可靠性度量。

Result: 综合实验表明，MATU能够有效估计跨不同任务和通信拓扑的整体且稳健的不确定性。

Conclusion: MATU填补了多智能体系统不确定性量化方法的空白，通过张量分解方法提供了全面、可推广的可靠性评估框架，解决了传统方法无法处理的MAS特有挑战。

Abstract: While Large Language Model-based Multi-Agent Systems (MAS) consistently outperform single-agent systems on complex tasks, their intricate interactions introduce critical reliability challenges arising from communication dynamics and role dependencies. Existing Uncertainty Quantification methods, typically designed for single-turn outputs, fail to address the unique complexities of the MAS. Specifically, these methods struggle with three distinct challenges: the cascading uncertainty in multi-step reasoning, the variability of inter-agent communication paths, and the diversity of communication topologies. To bridge this gap, we introduce MATU, a novel framework that quantifies uncertainty through tensor decomposition. MATU moves beyond analyzing final text outputs by representing entire reasoning trajectories as embedding matrices and organizing multiple execution runs into a higher-order tensor. By applying tensor decomposition, we disentangle and quantify distinct sources of uncertainty, offering a comprehensive reliability measure that is generalizable across different agent structures. We provide comprehensive experiments to show that MATU effectively estimates holistic and robust uncertainty across diverse tasks and communication topologies.

</details>


### [147] [EngageTriBoost: Predictive Modeling of User Engagement in Digital Mental Health Intervention Using Explainable Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08589)
*Ha Na Cho,Daniel Eisenberg,Cheryl King,Kai Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究利用机器学习预测数字心理健康干预工具的用户参与度，通过Ensemble模型达到84%准确率，并应用SHAP分析识别影响参与的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 年轻人心理健康问题日益严重，数字心理健康干预工具虽有潜力但面临采纳障碍，包括初始使用率低和高流失率，需要理解用户参与模式以改善采纳效果。

Method: 使用机器学习分析eBridge平台用户行为模式，开发Ensemble模型EngageTriBoost预测用户参与度（通过登录和咨询师互动衡量），并应用SHAP分析提供可解释的洞察。

Result: EngageTriBoost模型在预测用户参与度方面达到84%准确率，SHAP分析识别出情绪失调和感知污名化等关键影响因素，这些因素对DMHI采纳有重要影响。

Conclusion: 研究表明可解释机器学习能有效理解用户参与数字心理健康干预工具的模式，为改善采纳和提升心理健康成果提供重要洞察。

Abstract: Mental health challenges among young adults, are on the rise, necessitating effective solutions such as digital mental health interventions (DMHIs). Despite their promise, DMHIs face significant adoption barriers, including low initial uptake and high dropout rates. This study leverages machine learning (ML) to analyze behavioral patterns of users of a DMHI, eBridge, designed to increase the utilization of professional mental health services among at-risk college students through motivational interviewing-based online counseling. Our ensemble model, EngageTriBoost, achieved up to 84% accuracy in predicting engagement, measured by sign-ins and counselor interactions. We then applied the Shapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP) analysis which provided clear, interpretable insights into key factors influencing user engagement such as emotional dysregulation and perceived stigma, highlighting their critical effect on DMHI adoption. This study demonstrates the power of explainable ML for better understanding user engagement with DMHI to improve their adoption and achievable impact on mental health outcomes.

</details>


### [148] [Reservoir observer enhanced with residual calibration and attention mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08592)
*Yichen Liu,Wei Xiao,Tianguang Chu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合残差校准和注意力机制的储层观测器改进方法，显著提升了非线性动力系统中未测量变量的推断精度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统储层观测器在不同输入变量下的性能差异较大，在最坏情况下可能影响可靠性，需要提升推断性能

Method: 将残差校准模块和注意力机制集成到储层观测器设计中：残差校准利用估计残差信息优化观测器输出；注意力机制利用数据的时间依赖性丰富储层内部动态表示

Result: 在典型混沌系统上的实验表明，该方法显著提高了推断精度，特别是改善了传统储层观测器在最坏情况下的表现；使用转移熵解释了输入依赖的观测差异原因及所提方法的有效性

Conclusion: 集成残差校准和注意力机制的储层观测器设计能够有效提升非线性动力系统中未测量变量的推断性能，特别是在传统方法表现不佳的情况下

Abstract: Reservoir observers provide a data-driven approach to the inference of unmeasured variables from observed ones for nonlinear dynamical systems. While previous studies have demonstrated wide applicability, their performance may vary considerably with different input variables, even compromising reliability in the worst cases. To enhance the performance of inference, we integrate residual calibration and attention mechanism into the reservoir observer design. The residual calibration module leverages information from the estimation residuals to refine the observer output, and the attention mechanism exploits the temporal dependencies of the data to enrich the representation of reservoir internal dynamics. Experiments on typical chaotic systems demonstrate that our method substantially improves inference accuracy, especially for the worst cases resulting from the traditional reservoir observers. We also invoke the notion of transfer entropy to explain the reason for the input-dependent observation discrepancy and the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [149] [HiFloat4 Format for Language Model Pre-training on Ascend NPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08826)
